Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于里德堡原子态的可扩展量子互连方案，无需光学腔即可实现远距离量子纠缠，纠缠生成速率超过每秒30万次，适用于中性原子量子计算架构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了实现可扩展的量子计算，需要在远距离量子比特之间高效分发纠缠，现有方法受限于速率和可扩展性。

Method: 利用中性原子里德堡态之间的强偶极-偶极相互作用，在无光学腔条件下实现静止量子比特与传播光子之间的纠缠生成，并优化协议以适应实际实验参数。

Result: 理论分析表明，使用镱原子的里德堡态可实现纠缠生成速率大于每秒30万次。

Conclusion: 该量子互连方案具有高生成速率、可扩展性和设计灵活性，为中性原子量子网络的发展提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Distributing entanglement between distant qubits is a crucial element of scalable quantum computing. Here, we describe a scalable quantum interconnect that generates remote entanglement at rates approaching those compatible with two-qubit gates of current neutral-atom quantum processors. The proposed approach exploits the strong dipole-dipole interactions between atomic Rydberg states to generate entanglement between stationary qubits and propagating photons, without the need for an optical cavity. We provide a thorough description of the optimal conditions for the developed entanglement-generation protocol for realistic experimental parameters and demonstrate that entanglement-generation rates $\gtrsim 3\times 10^5$ s$^{-1}$ can be achieved using Rydberg states of ytterbium atoms. Given the inherent scalability and design flexibility of the proposed interconnect, our results suggest a promising approach towards distributed networks based on neutral-atom quantum architectures.

</details>


<div id='physics.soc-ph'></div>

# physics.soc-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [134] [Efficient generation of networks with minimal average shortest-path distance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04131)
*Meritxell Vila-Miñana,Filippo Radicchi*

Main category: physics.soc-ph

TL;DR: 提出了一种快速构建近似最优网络结构的算法，用于在给定度序列下最小化平均最短路径长度，尤其适用于大规模系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在网络设计中最小化距离并满足结构约束是一个核心问题，但在允许环存在时，寻找给定度序列下平均最短路径最小的网络结构变得计算困难。

Method: 首先连接高阶节点，然后按照标准配置模型添加额外连接，以构造近似解。

Result: 该算法在合成和真实度序列上均表现出色，尤其在中等异质性的合成序列上效果显著；在真实网络中平均缩短20%的全对最短路径，并与模拟退火结果接近但计算成本更低。

Conclusion: 所提算法简单高效，能为大规模系统提供接近最优的低距离网络结构，是实际应用中的可靠选择。

Abstract: Designing networks that minimize distances and satisfy structural constraints is a fundamental task across transportation, communication, and biological systems. Here, we consider the problem of finding, for a given degree sequence, the network structure displaying the smallest possible average shortest-path length. While exact solutions are available in linear time for trees, such an optimization problem becomes computationally infeasible as soon as loops are allowed in the networks. We propose a fast algorithm to construct approximate solutions to such a degree-constrained distance-minimization problem. Accordingly, edges are first created between high-degree nodes; then, additional connections are placed following the rules of the standard configuration model. In spite of its simplicity, the algorithm displays outstanding performance as demonstrated in our systematic experiments on both synthetic and real degree sequences. Our method is particularly effective on synthetic degree sequences displaying medium levels of heterogeneity. When applied to degree sequences of real networks, the proposed algorithm is able to reduce the all-pair shortest path of real structures by 20%, on average. We perform a validation on small-sized networks, where we compare the shortest-path distance of the networks generated with our algorithm against those obtained via simulated annealing optimization. Although simulated annealing yields slightly better structures, our proposed algorithm provides nearly identical solutions at a substantially lower computational cost, making it a solid method in applications concerning large-scale systems.

</details>


### [135] [Exact moment equivalence and structural nonidentifiability in nonlinear epidemics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04943)
*Roni Muslim*

Main category: physics.soc-ph

TL;DR: 在有限种群的SIS和SIR模型中，群体规模分布仅通过与非线性传播核阶数匹配的一组有限矩影响动力学，导致不同分布可能产生相同的流行病动态，从而限制了从聚合数据中唯一推断真实群体结构的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究在聚合流行病数据中，为何临时群体的传播无法保留完整的群体规模分布信息，并探讨群体分布对流行病动力学的影响机制。

Method: 基于有限种群中的SIS和SIR模型，分析群体规模分布通过其矩（moments）如何影响非线性传播动力学，并结合主方程求解与随机模拟验证理论预测。

Result: 发现群体规模分布仅通过与传播核阶数对应的有限矩影响动态；当相关矩相同时，不同分布可产生相同的随机动力学，包括瞬态演化、波动、灭绝时间统计和最终爆发规模；在确定性极限下，一阶矩决定入侵阈值，二阶矩控制相变性质及双稳态与滞后现象的出现；只有当高阶传播机制激活首个不匹配矩时，分布才可区分。

Conclusion: 流行病推断存在内在局限：单一聚合动力学协议只能识别一类等效的群体规模分布，无法唯一重建完整分布。

Abstract: Transmission through temporary groups does not necessarily preserve complete information about the group-size distribution in aggregate epidemic data. We show that, in finite-population SIS and SIR models, the group-size distribution enters the dynamics only through a finite set of moments selected by the order of the nonlinear transmission kernel. Consequently, markedly different distributions can generate identical stochastic dynamics when their relevant moments coincide. This equivalence extends to transient evolution, fluctuations, extinction-time statistics, and final outbreak sizes. In the deterministic limit, the first moment sets the invasion threshold, whereas the second controls the nature of the transition and the emergence of bistability and hysteresis. The two distributions become distinguishable only when a higher-order transmission mechanism activates their first unmatched moment; even a weak additional channel can shift the phase boundary and place the systems in different dynamical regimes. Solutions of the master equation and stochastic simulations support these analytical predictions. These results establish an intrinsic limit on epidemic inference: a single aggregate dynamical protocol can identify only an equivalence class of group-size distributions, rather than uniquely reconstructing the full distribution.

</details>


<div id='cs.CE'></div>

# cs.CE [[Back]](#toc)

### [136] [Simulation-Based Imaging: Learning Acoustic Inverse Problems from Simulated Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04145)
*Luke Bodmer,E. Bruce Pitman*

Main category: cs.CE

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于模拟的成像（SBI）框架，利用纯模拟数据训练的机器学习模型实现实时无损声学成像，具备硬件简单、成本低、可部署性强的潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决传统声学成像依赖真实标注数据和复杂硬件的问题，探索仅通过高保真模拟数据训练实现可靠逆问题求解的可能性。

Method: 使用高保真节点间断伽辽金（nodal Discontinuous Galerkin）前向求解器在单位立方体内随机生成包含体几何，生成大量模拟训练数据；训练一个2D卷积神经网络，直接从边界压力测量值映射到内部32×32×32体素的三维重建结果。

Result: 模型能准确恢复包含体的位置和尺寸，无需先验的包含体数量或几何信息；在5%加性测量噪声下重建误差仅增加13%；仅使用17%传感器（24/144）时性能下降仅4%，仍保持高质量重建。

Conclusion: SBI是一种可行的成像概念验证装置，其复杂性集中在软件而非硬件，为低成本、便携式、可部署的成像系统提供了新路径。

Abstract: We introduce Simulation-Based Imaging (SBI), a framework for non-destructive acoustic imaging in which machine learning models trained entirely on simulated data serve as real-time solvers for the acoustic inverse problem. A high-fidelity nodal Discontinuous Galerkin forward solver generates large training datasets by randomizing inclusion geometry within a unit-cube domain; a 2D convolutional neural network then learns a direct mapping from boundary pressure measurements to a 32 by 32 by 32 voxel reconstruction of the interior. The trained model reliably recovers inclusion position and size from 144 boundary sensors with no prior knowledge of inclusion count or geometry. Reconstruction error degrades by only 13% under 5% additive measurement noise, and just 17% of the sensor array (24 of 144 sensors) suffices for quality within 4% of full coverage. These results establish SBI as a viable proof-of-concept imaging device whose complexity resides in software rather than hardware, opening a path toward cheap, portable, deployable imaging systems.

</details>


### [137] [Trie-Constrained Token Prediction with Hierarchy-Aware Semantic Alignment for HS Code Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04464)
*Minseop Kim,Taekhyun Park,Kikun Park,Hyerim Bae*

Main category: cs.CE

TL;DR: 本研究提出TRIE-HSA方法，结合trie约束生成和层次语义对齐，提升小语言模型在商品文本到HS编码预测任务中的准确性和结构有效性，适用于资源受限的港口物流环境。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型虽在HS编码预测中表现良好，但计算成本高，难以在资源受限的场景（如集装箱码头）部署；小语言模型则易生成无效编码且忽略HS编码的层级语义结构。

Method: 提出TRIE-HSA方法，采用trie树约束小语言模型仅生成符合HS分类体系的有效编码，并通过层次感知语义对齐机制将商品文本表示与HS编码的层级结构对齐。

Result: 在真实港口数据上的实验表明，TRIE-HSA在HS6级别准确率上比零样本推理提升49.96个百分点，超过最强特定任务基线11.94个百分点。

Conclusion: TRIE-HSA能在参数少于100亿的情况下实现高精度且结构有效的HS编码预测，为无法支持大模型的港口物流场景提供了可行的部署方案。

Abstract: Harmonized System (HS) code prediction (HSP) from commodity text is essential to international trade, and its importance continues to grow in port logistics. For the purposes of such prediction, recently, large language models (LLMs) have been actively investigated, owing especially to their strong language-understanding capabilities. However, their high computational cost limits deployment in constrained environments such as container terminals. Small language models (SLMs) offer a practical alternative, but their smaller scale makes them prone to generating invalid HS codes and to overlooking the hierarchical semantics between commodity text and HS codes. To address these limitations, this study proposes TRIE-HSA, which combines trie-constrained token prediction with hierarchy-aware semantic alignment (HSA). This method constrains the SLM to predict only valid digits under the HS taxonomy and aligns commodity text representations with the hierarchical structure of HS codes. In extensive experiments on data collected from an operational container terminal, TRIE-HSA improved average HS6 accuracy by 49.96 %p over zero-shot inference and exceeded the strongest task-specific benchmark by 11.94 %p. These results demonstrate that accurate and structurally valid HSP is achievable with fewer than 10 billion parameters. Therefore, TRIE-HSA offers a practical basis for deployment of HSP in port logistics operations that cannot support largescale LLMs.

</details>


### [138] [Benchmarking Multi-fidelity Neural Operators on Complex PDE Problems with Non-trivial Fidelity Differences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04708)
*Ghifari Adam Faza,Kemas Zakaria,Pramudita Satria Palar,Keivan Shariatmadar,Lavi Rizki Zuhal,Hans Hallez,David Moens*

Main category: cs.CE

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了多种多保真度神经算子方法在四类偏微分方程（PDE）问题中的表现，特别引入了具有复杂低-高保真度模型差异的新测试案例，以更真实地模拟现实场景。研究发现，直接将低保真预测输入到高保真模型的方法在差异较大时易导致误差放大，而迁移学习通过利用低保真数据初始化权重并用高保真数据微调， consistently 提升精度，是构建高效多保真PDE代理模型的可靠方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于高保真（HF）数据获取成本高昂，神经算子训练常受限于数据量；现有研究多关注网格分辨率差异下的多保真学习，但实际应用中（如CFD中的RANS与LES模型）存在更复杂的模型差异，需更贴近现实的评估框架。

Method: 采用系统性评估方法，比较两步法、残差法、中间融合法和迁移学习等多种多保真神经算子策略，在四个PDE测试案例上进行实验，其中包括两个标准网格问题、一个具有方程差异的改进达西流问题和一个具有时间动态的非定常烟流问题，特别设计后两者以模拟超越网格差异的复杂LF-HF不匹配。

Result: 发现直接将低保真输出作为输入的策略在大差异下易引发误差放大，尤其在自回归设置中；而迁移学习通过使用低保真预训练权重初始化高保真模型，并结合少量高保真数据进行微调，表现出更优且稳定的精度提升。

Conclusion: 迁移学习是一种高效且可靠的多保真PDE代理建模方法，尤其适用于存在显著模型差异的复杂场景，应被确立为该领域的首选策略。

Abstract: Solving PDE-governed physical problems is computationally expensive, limiting the availability of high-fidelity (HF) data for training neural operators, which typically require large datasets. Multi-fidelity learning addresses this by combining abundant low-fidelity (LF) data with limited HF samples. To evaluate several multi-fidelity neural operator strategies, including two-step, residual, intermediate, and transfer learning, we conduct a systematic assessment across four PDE test cases: two standard grid-based problems, a modified Darcy flow with LF-HF governing equation discrepancies, and an unsteady smoke inflow with temporal dynamics. Most prior studies focus on grid-based LF-HF differences, but real-world discrepancies, such as those between RANS and LES models in CFD, can be more complex. To capture these more challenging scenarios, we introduce two new test cases specifically designed to emulate nontrivial LF-HF mismatch beyond simple grid-resolution differences, enabling a more realistic assessment of multi-fidelity strategies under substantial model discrepancies. We find that methods directly feeding LF predictions into HF models are prone to error amplification under large LF-HF discrepancies, particularly in autoregressive settings. In contrast, transfer learning, which uses LF-trained weights to initialise the HF model, consistently improves accuracy by providing a robust prior refined with HF data. These results highlight the importance of establishing transfer learning as a reliable approach for efficient multi-fidelity PDE surrogate modelling.

</details>


### [139] [scikit-rom: An Open-Source Python Platform for Teaching and Prototyping Projection-Based Reduced-Order Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04960)
*Suparno Bhattacharyya,Ali Syed,Jian Tao,Jean C. Ragusa*

Main category: cs.CE

TL;DR: 本文介绍了scikit-rom，一个用于投影型降阶模型（ROM）的开源Python库，旨在提升教学透明度与研究可修改性，支持从快照生成到超减法的完整流程，并提供多种超减法策略和示例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的ROM软件框架通常将多阶段流程抽象化或依赖难以调试的编译堆栈，不利于教学与原型开发，因此需要一个透明、模块化且适合交互式学习的工具。

Method: 基于轻量级有限元后端scikit-fem构建scikit-rom，采用模块化设计实现完整的投影型ROM流程，包括快照生成、SVD/POD基构造、Galerkin投影、离线-在线分解、超减法及误差评估，并集成四种超减法策略：DEIM、S-OPT、ECSW和ECM型求积。

Result: 提供了四个逐步深入的示例，涵盖线性、非线性、静态和瞬态问题，验证了该库在教学和研究原型中的实用性；代码、文档和示例公开可用。

Conclusion: scikit-rom为降阶建模的教学、工作坊和研究原型提供了一个透明、可扩展且易于使用的平台，显著降低了学习和开发门槛。

Abstract: Projection-based reduced-order modeling (ROM) has become a cornerstone technique for accelerating parameter-intensive engineering simulations, yet the methodology remains challenging to teach and prototype. The difficulty stems from its multi-stage workflow, which encompasses snapshot generation, SVD/POD basis construction, Galerkin projection, offline-online decomposition, hyper-reduction, and error assessment. Existing software frameworks either abstract these stages behind high-level interfaces or depend on compiled, monolithic solver stacks that are difficult for students and early-stage researchers to inspect or modify. This paper introduces scikit-rom, an open-source Python library designed to make the complete projection-based ROM pipeline transparent, modifiable, and suitable for interactive notebook-based exploration. Built on the lightweight finite element backend scikit-fem, scikit-rom provides modular problem templates and dedicated facilities for snapshot generation, reduced-basis construction, reduced-operator assembly, online ROM solution, hyper-reduction, and quantitative accuracy assessment within a unified workflow. Hyper-reduction is supported through four strategies: the Discrete Empirical Interpolation Method (DEIM), S-OPT sampling, the Energy-Conserving Sampling and Weighting (ECSW) scheme, and ECM-style cubature construction. Four progressive worked examples guide the reader from full-order simulation to ROM and hyper-reduced model construction across linear, nonlinear, static, and transient problem classes. The library is intended for graduate-level instruction, intensive workshops, and research prototyping in the reduced-order modeling community. Source code and examples are available at github.com/suparnob100/scikit-rom, with documentation at scikitrom.github.io.

</details>


<div id='eess.SY'></div>

# eess.SY [[Back]](#toc)

### [140] [Intrinsic Stochastic Successive Convexification on SE(3) for Chance Constrained 6-DOF Rendezvous](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04114)
*Fabio D'Onofrio,Renato Zanetti*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 提出了一种在SE(3)群上构建的内在随机连续凸化方法，用于六自由度航天器交会轨迹优化，能够联合优化标称轨迹、协方差和反馈律，提升概率约束满足性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统方法常将位置与姿态分开处理，或仅在确定性参考轨迹生成后考虑随机扰动，难以有效应对依赖于完整相对位姿的安全约束问题。

Method: 将随机连续凸化方法扩展到SE(3)流形，实现对刚体位姿轨迹的协方差引导与机会约束优化，捕捉平移与旋转运动不确定性之间的内在耦合。

Result: 数值仿真表明，该方法能有效塑造闭环散布特性，在满足碰撞规避、对接走廊、视场角及力/力矩概率约束方面优于传统反馈线性化跟踪方法。

Conclusion: 基于SE(3)的随机连续凸化框架为六自由度航天器交会提供了一种统一且高效的轨迹优化方案，特别适用于强耦合、高精度与高安全性的任务场景。

Abstract: This work presents an intrinsic stochastic successive convexification method formulated on the Special Euclidean group SE(3) for six degrees of freedom spacecraft rendezvous trajectory optimization. The proposed approach extends stochastic successive convexification, originally developed for Euclidean state spaces, to the nonlinear manifold of SE(3), thereby enabling a consistent covariance steering and chance constrained optimization of rigid body pose trajectories. While conventional trajectory optimization methods often treat position and attitude separately, or account for stochastic dispersion only after a deterministic reference trajectory has been generated, the proposed SE(3)-based formulation captures the intrinsic coupling between translational and rotational motion uncertainty. This coupling is especially important for rendezvous problems with safety constraints that depend on the full relative pose, including collision avoidance, docking corridor, camera field of view, and probabilistic force and torque bounds. Numerical simulations show that jointly optimizing the nominal trajectory, covariance, and feedback law shapes the closed loop dispersion and improves probabilistic constraint satisfaction relative to tracking a deterministic reference with a feedback linearization controller.

</details>


### [141] [Structured Differentiable Optimization for Efficient Decision-focused Learning in Power Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04189)
*Wangkun Xu,Fei Teng*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出了DiffAPQP，一个支持可扩展决策导向学习（DfL）的求解器灵活框架，专用于处理仿射参数化二次规划问题，首次在IEEE 118节点系统上实现了端到端的DfL训练，并显著提升了训练速度与内存效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的决策导向学习（DfL）在应用于实际电力系统时受限于训练过程中反复求解和微分大规模优化问题的高计算成本，亟需一种高效、可扩展的解决方案。

Method: 提出DiffAPQP框架：通过CVXPY自动将电力系统二次模型标准化为可微表达形式；利用求解器热启动和数据更新加速前向传播；基于KKT系统简化和包络定理推导高效反向传播梯度，避免求解伴随KKT系统。

Result: 在IEEE 118节点系统24小时调度场景中，相比CvxpyLayers，DiffAPQP实现2.27–3.58倍闭环和3.62–4.38倍反事实训练加速，最优配置下分别达3.91倍和6.39倍加速，同时内存峰值降低约50%。

Conclusion: DiffAPQP显著提升了DfL在大型电力系统中的训练效率与可扩展性，为复杂能源系统中决策导向建模的实际应用提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Decision-focused learning (DfL) trains forecasting models to align downstream decision consequences, such as power-system operating costs. However, its application to realistic power networks is limited by the need to repeatedly solve and differentiate large optimization problems during training. This paper presents DiffAPQP, a solver-flexible framework and open-source Python package for scalable DfL with affine-parametric quadratic programs. To accelerate the forward pass, DiffAPQP automatically canonicalizes quadratic power-system models written in CVXPY into a differentiation-ready representation and takes advantage of the repetitive solving structure through solver warm-start and solver-data update during training. For the backward pass acceleration, we establish the equivalence between differentiation through the full KKT system and a reduced system obtained by eliminating inactive inequality constraints. For training losses depending solely on the optimal value, we further derive an envelope-theorem-based gradient that avoids solving an adjoint KKT system, resulting in eligible backward time. To our knowledge, this work presents the first solver-based end-to-end DfL demonstration on the IEEE 118-bus system with a 24-hour coupled economic-dispatch and redispatch horizon. Under matched SCS and Clarabel backends on a Linux machine, DiffAPQP achieves $2.27\times$--$3.58\times$ closed-loop and $3.62\times$--$4.38\times$ counterfactual end-to-end DfL training speedups over CvxpyLayers. The best solver configurations increase these speedups to $3.91\times$ (from 38.65 to 9.55 min/epoch) and $6.39\times$ (from 10.73 to 1.68 min/epoch), respectively. Additionally, DiffAPQP reduces peak memory usage by approximately $50\%$, while keeping similar operating costs as CvxpyLayers.

</details>


### [142] [Real-time Assessment of Distribution Grid Security through Adaptive Smart Meter Measurements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04253)
*Jiaqi Chen,Line A. Roald*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 提出一种基于智能电表数据的实时安全评估方法，通过迭代算法自适应选择关键测量节点，确保配电网电压幅值在安全范围内。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布式能源的快速发展增加了配电网运行的不确定性和变异性，导致电压质量问题，而现有感知与通信能力有限，难以实现全面状态感知。

Method: 利用智能电表中已有的实时电压幅值数据，设计一种迭代算法，结合优化问题求解最差电压情况及其灵敏度信息，动态更新测量节点集合。

Result: 在IEEE 123节点系统上的测试表明，该算法能持续准确识别并跟踪电压最高和最低的节点，即使负荷随时间变化仍保持良好性能。

Conclusion: 所提算法能够以有限的实时测量有效实现配电网电压安全的实时评估，提升系统可观测性与运行安全性。

Abstract: The rapid expansion of distributed energy resources is heightening uncertainty and variability in distribution system operations, potentially leading to power quality challenges such as voltage magnitude violations and excessive voltage unbalance. Ensuring the dependable and secure operation of distribution grids requires system real-time assessment. However, constraints in sensing, measurement, and communication capabilities within distribution grids result in limited awareness of the system's state. To achieve better real-time estimates of distribution system security, we propose a real-time security assessment based on data from smart meters, which are already prevalent in most distribution grids. Assuming that it is possible to obtain a limited number of voltage magnitude measurements in real time, we design an iterative algorithm to adaptively identify a subset of smart meters whose real-time measurements allow us to certify that all voltage magnitudes remain within bounds. This algorithm iterates between (1) solving optimization problems to determine the worst possible voltage magnitudes, given a limited set of voltage magnitude measurements, and (2) leveraging the solutions and sensitivity information from these problems to update the measurement set. Numerical tests on the IEEE 123 distribution feeder demonstrate that the proposed algorithm consistently identifies and tracks the nodes with the highest and lowest voltage magnitude, even as the load changes over time.

</details>


### [143] [Contingency Detection Integrated Model Predictive Control for Resilient Load Frequency Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04370)
*Erfan Mehdipour Abadi,Hamid Varmazyari,Sharaf K. Magableh,Caisheng Wang,Le Yi Wang,Feng Lin*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 提出了一种结合扰动感知的故障检测与预测频率调节的CDI-MPC框架，用于提升含故障事件的电力系统负荷频率控制性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 故障会改变电力系统动态特性，并在模型预测控制（MPC）中引起预测失配，而故障后的动态模型通常无法及时获取。

Method: 将故障建模为随机混合系统（SHS）中的随机离散事件，设计一种扰动感知残差机制，联合识别系统模式并估计未知扰动，并将检测结果用于更新MPC预测模型。

Result: 仿真结果表明该方法能准确检测故障，并在多种故障场景和未知扰动下显著提升闭环负荷频率控制性能。

Conclusion: 所提出的CDI-MPC框架有效减少了由故障引起的预测误差，增强了LFC在动态变化运行条件下的鲁棒性与控制性能。

Abstract: Contingencies can alter power-system dynamics and introduce prediction mismatch in model predictive control (MPC)-based load frequency control (LFC). Although such events may be detected or cleared by protection systems, the corresponding post-contingency dynamic model may not be available to the MPC controller on the LFC time scale. This paper proposes a contingency detection-integrated MPC (CDI-MPC) framework that combines disturbance-aware contingency detection with predictive frequency regulation. Contingencies are modeled as stochastic discrete events of a stochastic hybrid system (SHS), and a disturbance-aware residual formulation is developed to jointly identify the active mode and estimate unknown disturbances. The detected mode is then used to update the MPC prediction model, reducing contingency-induced prediction mismatch under changing operating conditions. Simulation results demonstrate accurate contingency detection and substantial improvements in closed-loop LFC performance under multiple contingency scenarios and unknown disturbances.

</details>


### [144] [Toward Integrating Adaptive Experience Replay and Online Uncertainty Estimation in Safe Actor-Critic Optimal Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04732)
*Mahshad Rastegarmoghaddam,Davoud Nikkhouy,Shima Samadzadeh*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种集成的actor-critic安全控制架构，将不确定性估计、屏障过滤和经验回放统一设计，在机器人导航任务中实现了在极端测试下零碰撞且稳定到达目标，显著优于模块分离配置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有安全强化学习方法通常将障碍物过滤、不确定性估计和经验回放作为独立模块处理，忽略了它们对学习与控制数据的共同影响，导致系统在感知噪声和干扰下安全性与性能下降。

Method: 提出一种集成架构：利用不确定性估计动态更新控制屏障函数中的障碍物几何；通过滤波干预和估计残差确定经验回放优先级；批评家（critic）从实际执行的动作而非名义动作进行学习。在二维机器人导航任务上实例化该架构，并与六种模块匹配的对照配置进行对比实验。

Result: 在乘数为6.0的极端压力测试中，集成配置在5个评估种子中均无碰撞并成功到达目标，平均成本为$7.63\pm0.44$，障碍物信念均方根误差为$3.52\pm0.55$ cm；消融版本虽无碰撞但仅在4/5种子中成功，平均成本更高且信念误差更大。理论方面给出了有限训练下的回放暴露边界和鲁棒屏障条件。

Conclusion: 实验结果支持在该基准任务上耦合估计、安全过滤与回放机制能显著提升安全性和性能；但更广泛的安全部署与收敛性结论仍需进一步研究。

Abstract: Safe actor-critic control often treats barrier filtering, uncertainty estimation, and experience replay as separate modules, even though each changes the data used for learning and control. We develop an integrated architecture in which the uncertainty estimate updates the obstacle geometry used by a control barrier function, filter interventions and estimation residuals determine replay priority, and the critic learns from the executed rather than nominal action. We instantiate the architecture on a two-dimensional robot-navigation task with corrupted obstacle measurements and compare six component-matched configurations under common training budgets, random seeds, sensor streams, exploration, and disturbances. Evaluation includes a moderate post-training test, an eleven-level perception-noise sweep, and an exploratory extreme-stress test at multiplier $6.0$. In the extreme test, the integrated configuration recorded no contacts and reached the goal in all five evaluation seeds. Its mean cost was $7.63\pm0.44$ and its obstacle-belief root-mean-square error was $3.52\pm0.55$ cm. The uncertainty-estimation ablation also recorded no contacts but reached the goal in four of five seeds, with mean cost $8.96\pm2.08$ and belief error $11.08\pm1.23$ cm. A finite-training bound clarifies replay exposure, and a robust barrier condition states the required estimation-error and feasibility assumptions. The results support coupling estimation, safety filtering, and replay on this benchmark; broader safety and convergence claims require further study.

</details>


### [145] [Analytical Prediction of Voltage Collapse in Current-Limited Grid-Forming Inverters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04740)
*Wenhao Lin,Robin Preece,Panagiotis N. Papadopoulos*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种分析框架，用于预测电网形成型逆变器在大扰动下激活圆形电流限制器（CCL）时的电网电压边界，并判断运行平衡是否以饱和稳定平衡点（satSEP）形式持续存在。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于GFM逆变器过电流能力有限，在大扰动期间必须进行电流限制；然而CCL的激活并不总是导致运行平衡消失，因此需要理论框架来准确预测其动态行为和稳定性边界。

Method: 将带有冻结防积分饱和的CCL-GFM逆变器建模为分段光滑系统，包含正常控制和电流限制两种模式，并通过边界平衡分岔（BEB）解释限流器的激活；引入一种动态模型连续化方法以避免积分器冻结导致的秩亏问题，并结合滤波电容建立等效电路，推导出CCL激活的上下电压边界的解析表达式。

Result: 得到了CCL激活时上下边界电压的闭式表达式；下边界斜率为正时，satSEP在电流限制模式下仍可维持，直至后续鞍结分岔才失稳；非正斜率则导致非光滑折叠及BEB处直接失衡；上边界斜率在给定参数条件下恒为负；通过功率角分析、单逆变器与改进9节点系统的动态模型连续化以及时域仿真验证了理论预测。

Conclusion: CCL的激活可能引起即时平衡丧失（非光滑折叠），也可能允许satSEP持续存在直至后续鞍结分岔才失去，具体取决于下边界斜率的符号，该分析框架可有效预测GFM逆变器在电流限制下的稳定行为。

Abstract: The limited overcurrent capability of grid-forming (GFM) inverters makes current limiting essential during large disturbances. Activation of a circular current limiter (CCL) does not always cause the operating equilibrium to disappear. This paper develops an analytical framework to predict the grid-voltage boundaries at which the CCL is activated, determine whether the operating equilibrium persists as a saturated stable equilibrium point (satSEP), and identify the voltage at which it is lost. The CCL-based GFM inverter with frozen anti-windup is formulated as a piecewise-smooth system comprising normal-control and current-limited modes, so limiter activation is interpreted as a boundary-equilibrium bifurcation (BEB). A continuation formulation that switches to a reduced current-limited model when the CCL is activated is introduced to avoid the rank deficiency caused by frozen integrator states. An equivalent circuit that includes the filter capacitor yields closed-form expressions for the lower and upper boundary voltages at which the CCL is activated. A positive lower-boundary slope predicts that a satSEP persists in the current-limited mode and is lost at a later saddle-node, whereas a nonpositive slope predicts a non-smooth fold and equilibrium loss at the BEB. The upper-boundary slope is always negative under the assumed parameter conditions. Power-angle analysis, dynamic-model continuation in single-inverter and modified 9-bus systems, and time-domain simulations validate these predictions, showing that CCL activation can either cause immediate equilibrium loss through a non-smooth fold or allow a satSEP to persist until it is lost at a later saddle-node.

</details>


### [146] [Prescribed Performance Leader-Following Consensus with Event-Based Broadcasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04743)
*Athanasios K. Gkesoulis,Haris E. Psillakis,George C. Karras,Charalampos P. Bechlioulis*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于事件触发广播的有向网络规定性能领导者-跟随者一致性协议，通过引入衰减的接收端性能校正项处理异步接收引起的误差跳变，并证明了系统解的存在唯一性、信号有界性及一致性误差的规定性能边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了在减少通信频率的同时保证有向网络中领导者-跟随者一致性的瞬态与稳态性能，需要设计一种能处理异步采样误差跳变并满足规定性能要求的事件触发控制机制。

Method: 采用事件触发广播机制，各节点仅根据当前状态和上次广播值决定发送时机；跟随者在广播间隔内保持邻居和领导者的采样值；为应对异步接收导致的邻域误差跳变，引入一个衰减型接收端性能校正项来动态调整规定性能函数。

Result: 对于包含时变领导者（轨迹及其导数有界）且具有生成树结构的有向网络，证明了系统存在唯一的非Zenon完整解，所有闭环信号有界，并给出了采样与连续邻域误差及跟踪误差的显式规定性能边界，量化了通信拓扑与广播阈值的影响。

Conclusion: 所提事件触发控制策略有效实现了规定性能下的领导者-跟随者一致性，兼顾通信效率与控制性能，理论分析与仿真验证了方法的有效性与鲁棒性。

Abstract: This paper develops a prescribed performance leader-following consensus protocol for directed networks under event-based broadcasting. Each node schedules its transmissions using only its current state and its last broadcast value, while followers use held neighbor and leader samples between broadcasts. To accommodate jumps in sampled neighborhood errors caused by asynchronous receptions, a decaying receiver-side performance correction term temporarily modifies the prescribed performance function. For a directed network containing a spanning tree rooted at a time-varying leader with bounded trajectory and derivative, we prove existence and uniqueness of complete non-Zeno solutions, boundedness of all closed-loop signals, and explicit prescribed bounds for the sampled and continuous neighborhood disagreements and the leader-following error. These bounds quantify the effects of the communication topology and broadcast thresholds. Simulations verify the prescribed performance bounds and illustrate the event-based communication mechanism.

</details>


### [147] [Transforming Remanufacturing Automation with Large Language Models: A Forward-Looking Analysis with Case Studies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04854)
*Chang Liu,Sara Behdad,Prabhakar Pagilla,Xiao Liang,Minghui Zheng*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文探讨了大型语言模型（LLM）在再制造自动化中的潜在作用，提出了一个名为ReManGPT的概念框架，并通过三个典型案例和应用场景展示了其在电池、电子废弃物和电机再制造中的应用前景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于废旧产品再制造过程高度依赖人工经验，且存在较大的变异性与不确定性，而大型语言模型具备处理非结构化数据和生成专家级输出的能力，因此探索LLM在该领域的应用具有重要意义。

Method: 通过对现有与再制造相关的LLM研究进行批判性综述，提出ReManGPT概念框架，并结合三个代表性案例和具体应用场景进行分析。

Result: ReManGPT框架能够有效支持再制造中的决策、人机交互与自动化控制；在电动汽车电池、电子废弃物和电动机的再制造中展现出解决领域特定问题的潜力。

Conclusion: LLM有望显著降低对专业人力的依赖，推动再制造智能化发展，但实际部署仍面临数据安全、模型可靠性与系统集成等挑战，未来需进一步研究LLM辅助操作与语言-动作模型在机器人自动化中的应用。

Abstract: With growing concerns about resource scarcity and environmental degradation, remanufacturing of end-of-life (EoL) products within the circular economy is attracting increasing attention. Remanufacturing can preserve most of the original manufacturing value and materials while transforming EoL products into like-new condition. However, the variability and uncertainty of EoL products make remanufacturing highly dependent on human expertise. Recently, large language models (LLMs) have demonstrated remarkable capabilities in learning from massive, unstructured datasets, generating expert-level output across various tasks, and communicating with humans in natural language for interpretation. These advantages can align closely with the complex demands of remanufacturing, thereby mitigating the reliance on specialized expertise. However, their roles and research progress in this domain remain underexplored. In this paper, we present a forward-looking review and analysis of the role of LLMs in remanufacturing automation, grounded in a brief critical review of existing LLM-related studies relevant to remanufacturing. Building on this foundation, we introduce ReManGPT as a conceptual framework and use three representative case studies to illustrate selected modules of the framework in practical remanufacturing scenarios. We also analyze three representative remanufacturing applications, electric vehicle batteries, electronic waste, and electric motors, to illustrate how the proposed framework could address their domain-specific challenges. Finally, we discuss the current barriers to deploying this framework in practice and outline future research directions, including LLM-assisted human operation and language-action models for robotic automation.

</details>


### [148] [Quantifying the Availability of Synchronized and Non-Synchronized Generating Units When Needed](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04963)
*Yufan Zhang,Feng Zhao*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种量化同步和非同步发电机组在需要时可用概率的方法，定义了SynFORd和NonSynFORd两个指标，并通过新英格兰系统数据验证了非同步机组通常具有更高且更分散的故障概率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电力系统运行中普遍认为同步机组比非同步机组更可能在需要时可用，但缺乏明确的定量衡量。本文旨在建立可量化的故障概率指标以区分两者可用性差异。

Method: 提出了针对同步和非同步发电机组的故障概率指标SynFORd和NonSynFORd，考虑其初始运行状态的不同，推导出闭式解析表达式，并分析主导故障机制。

Result: 案例研究显示，非同步机组的故障概率普遍高于同步机组，且分布更分散；所提指标能有效反映两类机组的可用性差异。

Conclusion: 同步机组在需要时更可能可用，非同步机组因启动和并网过程导致更高的故障风险，应在其可靠性评估中加以区别对待。

Abstract: Do synchronized units have higher probabilities of being available when needed than non-synchronized units? Power system operation implicitly relies on the qualitative belief that synchronized units are more likely to be available when needed because they are already synchronized to the grid, whereas non-synchronized units must first start and synchronize before becoming available. However, this distinction is rarely expressed through an explicit quantitative measure. To quantify this distinction, we propose failure probabilities for synchronized and non-synchronized generating units, denoted by SynFORd and NonSynFORd, by accounting for their different initial operating states. The complements of the proposed probabilities directly represent the corresponding availability probabilities when the units are needed. Closed-form analytical expressions are derived, revealing the dominant failure mechanisms of the two unit types. Case studies using generating-unit data from the New England system show that non-synchronized units generally exhibit higher and more dispersed failure probabilities than synchronized units.

</details>


### [149] [ORACLE: A Multi-Objective Reinforcement Learning-Based Analog Circuit Design Optimizer with Large Language Models-Guided Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04999)
*Osei Brempong,Mohammed Ayman Habib,Vivan Poddar,Morteza Fayazi*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: ORACLE是一个基于强化学习的开源多目标模拟电路设计优化框架，采用向量值学习和偏好感知条件机制，支持单一模型在不同权衡设置下生成设计，无需重新训练，并显著提升运行效率和设计性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于强化学习的模拟电路设计方法多为单目标优化，或多目标问题被简化为标量奖励，难以捕捉真实帕累托权衡，且每次设计需求变化需重新训练模型，限制了实用性。

Method: 提出ORACLE框架，使用向量值奖励替代标量奖励，引入偏好向量表示多目标权重，并设计归一化权重引导和余弦对齐引导策略提升收敛性；结合大语言模型指导动作选择，过滤可能导致次优设计或增加运行时间的动作。

Result: 在2,000个测试用例上，ORACLE相比现有最先进方法将运行时间减少20.4倍至104.4倍，满足99.9%的目标规格要求，输出指标表现提升5.1倍至318.6倍。

Conclusion: ORACLE实现了真正的多目标模拟电路自动化设计，支持灵活偏好调节、高效优化和高规格达标率，显著优于传统标量奖励方法，具有良好的实用性和扩展性。

Abstract: Analog circuit design automation using reinforcement learning (RL) has emerged as a promising approach for reducing manual effort. However, many existing RL-based methods focus on single-objective optimization. Even methods designed for multi-objective (MO) problems often reduce multiple design specifications to a single scalar reward. This simplification limits the ability to capture the true Pareto trade-off among competing objectives and often leads to suboptimal designs. Moreover, requiring the model to be retrained from scratch whenever the desired MO specifications change remains a key limitation. To address these challenges, we present ORACLE, an open-source RL-based framework for MO analog circuit design optimization that replaces scalar reward optimization with vector-valued learning and preference-aware conditioning. ORACLE represents a true MO analog circuit design optimizer that uses a preference vector to specify the relative weights of multiple objectives, enabling a single trained model to generate designs across diverse trade-off settings without retraining. We further propose two preference-guidance strategies, namely normalized-weight guidance and cosine-aligned guidance, to improve convergence. In addition, we incorporate a large language model (LLM)-guided action selection mechanism to filter actions that are likely to lead to suboptimal designs or increased runtime. Our results show that, on multiple circuit topologies with 2,000 test cases, ORACLE reduces runtime by 20.4x - 104.4x compared to state-of-the-art approaches. It also meets 99.9% of the 2,000 target specifications, and achieves 5.1x - 318.6x better figure of merit in the resulting output specs.

</details>


### [150] [Exact Model-Free Policy Iteration for Co-safe LTL Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.05047)
*Zetong Xuan,Yu Wang*

Main category: eess.SY

TL;DR: 提出了一种新的两步无模型强化学习方法，用于解决有限马尔可夫决策过程中co-safe LTL目标下的最大可达性问题，通过折扣代理和无折扣策略评估确保收敛到最优策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对co-safe LTL目标的无模型强化学习中，传统Q学习等方法因贝尔曼方程解的非唯一性和非收缩性可能导致不收敛。

Method: 首先使用折扣代理识别消除解非唯一性的clamp集，然后在该集上应用无折扣策略评估与贪婪策略改进。

Result: 证明了策略评估步骤的几乎必然收敛性和策略迭代算法在最优策略上的有限步终止性，并通过随机网格世界实验验证了理论结果。

Conclusion: 所提两步法能有效解决贝尔曼方程非唯一性导致的收敛问题，保证找到最优策略。

Abstract: This work studies model-free reinforcement learning for co-safe linear temporal logic (sc-LTL) objectives in finite Markov decision processes, which can be reduced to maximal reachability objectives via the standard product construction. For this problem, direct sample-based bootstrap methods (e.g., TD or Q-learning) may fail to converge to optimal policies due to the noncontractive nature and nonuniqueness of solutions to the Bellman equation. We develop a new two-step model-free reinforcement learning method that first uses a discounted surrogate to identify a clamp set that resolves this nonuniqueness, and then applies undiscounted policy evaluation and greedy policy improvement with guarantees of finding an optimal solution. We prove almost-sure convergence of the policy evaluation step and finite termination of the policy iteration algorithm at an optimal policy. These theoretical results are validated through numerical experiments on a stochastic grid world.

</details>


<div id='physics.geo-ph'></div>

# physics.geo-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [151] [Regional Chaos Synchronization towards controlling high-dimensional open environmental systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04301)
*Yohei Sawada*

Main category: physics.geo-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种区域混沌同步的新框架，用于解决高维开放混沌系统（如天气控制）中的实际控制问题，通过在目标区域内选择合适的轨迹以减少外部误差注入，从而实现有效的局部同步。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统全局混沌同步无法适用于高维开放系统（如天气），因为在全域内使驱动与响应系统趋于一致在实践中不可行，因此需要一种更实用的局部控制方法。

Method: 提出了区域混沌同步的概念，仅在目标区域内使用执行器将状态变量与目标轨迹同步，并通过理论分析和数值实验研究不同轨迹下同步性能的差异及外部误差的影响。

Result: 发现区域混沌同步的性能受外部区域误差注入的显著影响，且不同目标轨迹下的同步效果存在差异；适当选择仅在目标区域内偏离自然轨迹的目标轨迹可有效降低外部误差影响，提升同步性能。

Conclusion: 区域混沌同步为高维开放混沌系统的实际控制提供了可行框架，尤其适用于天气调控等现实应用场景，其关键在于优化目标轨迹以抑制跨区域误差传播。

Abstract: Although controlling chaos can be regarded as a form of chaos synchronization, conventional chaos synchronization does not provide a practical framework for controlling high-dimensional open chaotic systems, such as weather. In high-dimensional open environmental systems, it is simply impossible to make the difference between drive and response systems asymptotically approach zero over the entire domain. Instead of conventional global chaos synchronization, here I define regional chaos synchronization, in which state variables within only a small target region are synchronized with a target trajectory using actuators located in the target region. Theoretical considerations and numerical experiments show that the performance of regional chaos synchronization is substantially degraded by the injection of errors from exterior regions that the controller is not intended to modify. Even under the same actuator specifications, the performances of regional chaos synchronization differ among trajectories, which is not the case of conventional global chaos synchronization. An appropriate target trajectory differs substantially from the natural trajectory only in the target region. This choice of target trajectories can minimize the injection of errors from exterior regions and thereby enable successful regional chaos synchronization. The implications of this new framework for realistic control problems such as weather control are discussed.

</details>


### [152] [Theoretical Framework for Phase-space Local-Angle-Domain Waveform Inversion in Anisotropic Elastic Media](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04901)
*Zvi Koren*

Main category: physics.geo-ph

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于局部角度域（LAD）的弹性全波形反演（FWI）相空间方法，通过在相空间中组织地震信息，改善参数分离、减少速度-反射率串扰，并提升反演稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统FWI在数据域中最小化波形残差，易受周期跳跃和参数串扰影响；而速度与阻抗扰动在相空间中分布区域不同，启发了该方法的提出。

Method: 利用位置、传播方向、散射角、方位角和时间参数化的相空间域，最小化像域中的不一致性；采用LAD表示法，结合Born线性化下的射线/束方法实现显式Hessian计算。

Result: Hessian矩阵更接近对角占优，增强了参数分离性，减少了速度-反射率串扰，提升了反演稳定性；同时支持自适应、分辨率驱动的反演，并缓解周期跳跃问题。

Conclusion: LAD-FWI提供了一个统一框架，整合了各向异性参数估计、转换波成像和绕射成像等功能，适用于多种正演引擎，为弹性FWI和高分辨率成像提供了物理意义明确的新路径。

Abstract: We present a phase-space formulation of elastic full-waveform inversion (FWI) based on local-angle-domain (LAD) representations of seismic scattering. Instead of minimizing acquisition-domain waveform misfits, the method minimizes image-domain inconsistencies in a phase-space domain parameterized by position, propagation direction, scattering angle, azimuth, and time. The formulation is motivated by the observation that velocity and impedance perturbations occupy largely distinct regions of phase space. For anisotropic elastic media, we derive a generalized objective function together with the corresponding adjoint-state gradient and Hessian operators. Representing LAD images through directional and opening wavenumbers provides a unified description of propagation and scattering effects. The approach is independent of the forward-modeling engine and can be implemented with finite-difference, finite-element, spectral-element, ray-based, or beam-based methods. Under Born linearization, ray/beam formulations allow explicit Hessian computation. Owing to the near-orthogonality of directional and opening-angle sensitivities, the Hessian is more diagonally dominant than in conventional FWI, improving parameter separation, reducing velocity-reflectivity crosstalk, and enhancing inversion stability. By organizing seismic information in a physically meaningful phase-space representation, LAD-FWI supports adaptive, resolution-driven inversion and may reduce cycle skipping through attributes that vary more smoothly than waveform residuals. The framework naturally integrates directional focusing, reflector-consistency analysis, anisotropic parameter estimation, converted-wave imaging, and diffraction-based characterization, providing a unified workflow for elastic FWI, migration-velocity analysis, impedance inversion, and high-resolution subsurface imaging.

</details>


### [153] [Modified Hamiltonian-driven shortest-path ray tracing in dissipative anisotropic media](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.04958)
*Muhammad F. T. Darwis,Fateh Bouchaala,Umair Bin Waheed,Mohamed Kamel Riahi*

Main category: physics.geo-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于g*-哈密顿量的最短路径射线追踪算法，用于层状耗散各向异性介质中的初至波模拟。通过三种核函数（OEV、C-RRT、MEV）计算复能量速度及相关射线参数，并比较其数值稳定性与物理一致性。结果表明，MEV核在qSV波建模中具有最佳稳定性，避免了非物理解和数值不稳定性，优于OEV和C-RRT方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统实射线追踪和复能量速度方法在处理qSV尖点、波前三分裂及突变间断时存在困难，且难以准确描述初至折射波以及波传播与能量耗散的耦合行为。现有改进方法依赖近似哈密顿体系，多验证于平滑模型，缺乏对复杂地质与流变间断条件下的稳健性分析。因此，需要一种能有效处理各向异性耗散介质中初至波传播的稳定射线追踪方法。

Method: 将g*-哈密顿量（MEV）引入基于最短路径的射线追踪算法中，在局部尺度上计算体波的复能量速度及其对应的射线速度、射线衰减和射线品质因子。采用三种不同核函数（原始复能量速度OEV、共轭实射线追踪C-RRT、g*-哈密顿量MEV）进行对比分析，并评估其在层状耗散各向异性介质中的数值表现与优化策略。

Result: 三种核在qSH和qP波上结果一致；OEV在qSV波中产生伪反射或数值不稳定及非物理解；C-RRT在部分qP和qSV走时中出现非物理负衰减，且射线品质因子严重偏移；MEV核驱动的最短路径算法在初至波行为上表现出最优的计算稳定性与物理合理性。

Conclusion: 基于g*-哈密顿量（MEV）的最短路径射线追踪方法在层状耗散各向异性介质中具有更高的数值稳定性与物理保真度，尤其适用于qSV波初至波模拟，克服了OEV和C-RRT方法的局限性，为地震波传播与能量耗散耦合建模提供了更可靠工具。

Abstract: Enhancing ray tracing in dissipative anisotropic media is challenging because the subsurface exhibits both geologic and rheological discontinuities and directionally dependent attenuation. Conventional real-ray tracing and complex energy-velocity formulations struggle with qSV cusps, wavefront triplications, and abrupt discontinuities. Recent conjugate and shortest-path extensions have been proposed to mitigate these issues, yet they rely on approximate Hamiltonian formulations that are typically tested in smooth background models. In addition, they rarely address first-arrival refraction or the coupled behavior of wave propagation and energy dissipation. Here, the g*-Hamiltonian-based formulation is incorporated into a shortest-path-based ray-tracing algorithm for layered dissipative anisotropic media. At the local scale, we first compute the complex energy velocities and corresponding ray quantities, which are ray velocity, ray attenuation, and ray quality factor, using three kernels. These kernels produce complex energy velocities for all body waves in different fashions, namely, the original complex energy velocity (OEV), conjugate real ray tracing (C-RRT), and g*-Hamiltonian (MEV). We then analyze their discrepancies and numerical behavior and examine several optimization strategies. The results demonstrate that all kernels agree for qSH and qP waves, whereas OEV generates pseudo-reflection or numerical instability and unphysical attenuation along ray paths in qSV modeling. C-RRT produces large errors, including non-physical negative attenuation in some qP and qSV traveltime values, and strongly biased ray quality factors under our parameter regime. Among the tested kernels, MEV-driven shortest-path ray tracing produces the most computationally stable first-arrival behavior.

</details>
